     1→/**
     2→ * Context Injector -- Pure functions for brain-context injection
     3→ *
     4→ * Responsible for:
     5→ * 1. Ranking context candidates by weighted cosine similarity
     6→ * 2. Selecting top N within token budget
     7→ * 3. Building <brain-context> XML block
     8→ * 4. Injecting into system prompt (string or array form)
     9→ *
    10→ * All functions are pure -- no IO, no side effects.
    11→ * The orchestration (embedding, cache, DB queries) lives in the proxy route.
    12→ */
    13→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
    14→import { extractSearchTerms } from "../graph/bm25-search";
    15→import { applyRrf, type RrfItem } from "../graph/rrf";
    16→
    17→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→// Local cosine similarity (kept for legacy rankCandidates; no external dep)
    19→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→
    21→function cosineSimilarity(a: number[], b: number[]): number {
    22→  if (a.length !== b.length || a.length === 0) return -1;
    23→  let dot = 0, normA = 0, normB = 0;
    24→  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
    25→    dot += a[i] * b[i];
    26→    normA += a[i] * a[i];
    27→    normB += b[i] * b[i];
    28→  }
    29→  if (normA === 0 || normB === 0) return -1;
    30→  return dot / (Math.sqrt(normA) * Math.sqrt(normB));
    31→}
    32→
    33→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→// Types
    35→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→export type ContextCandidate = {
    38→  readonly id: string;
    39→  readonly type: "decision" | "learning" | "observation";
    40→  readonly text: string;
    41→  readonly embedding?: number[];
    42→  readonly weight: number;
    43→};
    44→
    45→export type Bm25ContextCandidate = {
    46→  readonly id: string;
    47→  readonly type: "decision" | "learning" | "observation";
    48→  readonly text: string;
    49→  readonly bm25Score: number;
    50→  readonly updatedAt: string;
    51→};
    52→
    53→export type RankedCandidate = {
    54→  readonly id: string;
    55→  readonly type: "decision" | "learning" | "observation";
    56→  readonly text: string;
    57→  readonly score: number;
    58→};
    59→
    60→export type InjectionResult = {
    61→  readonly system: string | Array<{ type: string; text: string; cache_control?: { type: string } }>;
    62→  readonly injected: boolean;
    63→  readonly tokensEstimated: number;
    64→  readonly decisionsCount: number;
    65→  readonly learningsCount: number;
    66→  readonly observationsCount: number;
    67→};
    68→
    69→type SystemPrompt = string | Array<{ type: string; text: string; cache_control?: { type: string } }> | undefined;
    70→
    71→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→// Recent Changes Types (04-01: vector search classification)
    73→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→export type RecentChangeCandidate = {
    76→  readonly id: string;
    77→  readonly table: "decision" | "task" | "observation";
    78→  readonly text: string;
    79→  readonly similarity: number;
    80→  readonly updatedAt: string;
    81→};
    82→
    83→export type ClassifiedChange = RecentChangeCandidate & {
    84→  readonly classification: "urgent-context" | "context-update";
    85→};
    86→
    87→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    88→// Token Estimation (pure, ~4 chars per token)
    89→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    90→
    91→export function estimateTokenCount(text: string): number {
    92→  return Math.ceil(text.length / 4);
    93→}
    94→
    95→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    96→// Ranking: weighted cosine similarity
    97→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98→
    99→export function rankCandidates(
   100→  candidates: ContextCandidate[],
   101→  queryEmbedding: number[],
   102→): RankedCandidate[] {
   103→  return candidates
   104→    .map((c) => {
   105→      const hasEmbedding = c.embedding !== undefined && c.embedding.length > 0;
   106→      const score = hasEmbedding
   107→        ? cosineSimilarity(c.embedding!, queryEmbedding) * c.weight
   108→        : c.weight * 0.5;
   109→      return { id: c.id, type: c.type, text: c.text, score };
   110→    })
   111→    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
   112→}
   113→
   114→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→// BM25 + Recency Ranking (02-01: pure functions)
   116→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→const DEFAULT_RECENCY_HALFLIFE_HOURS = 168; // 1 week
   119→
   120→/**
   121→ * Compute exponential recency decay factor.
   122→ * decayFactor = exp(-ageHours / halflife)
   123→ *
   124→ * Returns 1.0 for zero or negative age (future timestamps treated as fresh).
   125→ */
   126→export function computeRecencyDecay(ageHours: number, halflife: number): number {
   127→  if (ageHours <= 0) return 1.0;
   128→  return Math.exp(-ageHours / halflife);
   129→}
   130→
   131→/**
   132→ * Compute final context score: BM25 relevance weighted by recency decay.
   133→ * finalScore = bm25Score * exp(-ageHours / halflife)
   134→ */
   135→export function computeFinalScore(
   136→  bm25Score: number,
   137→  updatedAt: string,
   138→  now: Date,
   139→  halflife: number,
   140→): number {
   141→  const ageMs = now.getTime() - new Date(updatedAt).getTime();
   142→  const ageHours = ageMs / (1000 * 60 * 60);
   143→  return bm25Score * computeRecencyDecay(ageHours, halflife);
   144→}
   145→
   146→/**
   147→ * Rank BM25 context candidates by combined relevance and recency.
   148→ * Pure pipeline: map to final scores, sort descending.
   149→ */
   150→export function rankByBm25WithRecency(
   151→  candidates: Bm25ContextCandidate[],
   152→  now: Date,
   153→  halflife: number = DEFAULT_RECENCY_HALFLIFE_HOURS,
   154→): RankedCandidate[] {
   155→  return candidates
   156→    .map((c) => ({
   157→      id: c.id,
   158→      type: c.type,
   159→      text: c.text,
   160→      score: computeFinalScore(c.bm25Score, c.updatedAt, now, halflife),
   161→    }))
   162→    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
   163→}
   164→
   165→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166→// Budget Selection: top N within token budget
   167→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   168→
   169→export function selectWithinBudget(
   170→  ranked: RankedCandidate[],
   171→  tokenBudget: number,
   172→): RankedCandidate[] {
   173→  const selected: RankedCandidate[] = [];
   174→  let tokensUsed = 0;
   175→
   176→  for (const candidate of ranked) {
   177→    const candidateTokens = estimateTokenCount(candidate.text);
   178→    if (tokensUsed + candidateTokens > tokenBudget) break;
   179→    selected.push(candidate);
   180→    tokensUsed += candidateTokens;
   181→  }
   182→
   183→  return selected;
   184→}
   185→
   186→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   187→// XML Block Construction
   188→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   189→
   190→export function buildBrainContextXml(selected: RankedCandidate[]): string {
   191→  if (selected.length === 0) return "";
   192→
   193→  const decisions = selected.filter((c) => c.type === "decision");
   194→  const learnings = selected.filter((c) => c.type === "learning");
   195→  const observations = selected.filter((c) => c.type === "observation");
   196→
   197→  const sections: string[] = [];
   198→
   199→  if (decisions.length > 0) {
   200→    const items = decisions.map((d) => `  <item>${d.text}</item>`).join("\n");
   201→    sections.push(`<decisions>\n${items}\n</decisions>`);
   202→  }
   203→
   204→  if (learnings.length > 0) {
   205→    const items = learnings.map((l) => `  <item>${l.text}</item>`).join("\n");
   206→    sections.push(`<learnings>\n${items}\n</learnings>`);
   207→  }
   208→
   209→  if (observations.length > 0) {
   210→    const items = observations.map((o) => `  <item>${o.text}</item>`).join("\n");
   211→    sections.push(`<observations>\n${items}\n</observations>`);
   212→  }
   213→
   214→  return `<brain-context>\n${sections.join("\n")}\n</brain-context>`;
   215→}
   216→
   217→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   218→// System Prompt Injection
   219→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   220→
   221→export function injectBrainContext(
   222→  originalSystem: SystemPrompt,
   223→  brainContextXml: string,
   224→): InjectionResult {
   225→  if (!brainContextXml) {
   226→    return {
   227→      system: originalSystem ?? "",
   228→      injected: false,
   229→      tokensEstimated: 0,
   230→      decisionsCount: 0,
   231→      learningsCount: 0,
   232→      observationsCount: 0,
   233→    };
   234→  }
   235→
   236→  const tokensEstimated = estimateTokenCount(brainContextXml);
   237→
   238→  // Count items in XML (by counting <item> tags per section)
   239→  const decisionsCount = (brainContextXml.match(/<decisions>[\s\S]*?<\/decisions>/)?.[0]?.match(/<item>/g) ?? []).length;
   240→  const learningsCount = (brainContextXml.match(/<learnings>[\s\S]*?<\/learnings>/)?.[0]?.match(/<item>/g) ?? []).length;
   241→  const observationsCount = (brainContextXml.match(/<observations>[\s\S]*?<\/observations>/)?.[0]?.match(/<item>/g) ?? []).length;
   242→
   243→  if (Array.isArray(originalSystem)) {
   244→    // Array-form: append brain-context as additional text block with cache_control: ephemeral
   245→    const enriched = [
   246→      ...originalSystem,
   247→      {
   248→        type: "text",
   249→        text: brainContextXml,
   250→        cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" },
   251→      },
   252→    ];
   253→
   254→    return {
   255→      system: enriched,
   256→      injected: true,
   257→      tokensEstimated,
   258→      decisionsCount,
   259→      learningsCount,
   260→      observationsCount,
   261→    };
   262→  }
   263→
   264→  if (typeof originalSystem === "string") {
   265→    // String-form: append brain-context after original text
   266→    return {
   267→      system: `${originalSystem}\n\n${brainContextXml}`,
   268→      injected: true,
   269→      tokensEstimated,
   270→      decisionsCount,
   271→      learningsCount,
   272→      observationsCount,
   273→    };
   274→  }
   275→
   276→  // No original system prompt: use brain-context as system prompt
   277→  return {
   278→    system: brainContextXml,
   279→    injected: true,
   280→    tokensEstimated,
   281→    decisionsCount,
   282→    learningsCount,
   283→    observationsCount,
   284→  };
   285→}
   286→
   287→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   288→// Recent Changes Injection into System Prompt (04-02: pure)
   289→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   290→
   291→/**
   292→ * Injects recent changes XML into a system prompt with correct ordering:
   293→ * - <urgent-context> is injected BEFORE <brain-context>
   294→ * - <context-update> is injected AFTER <brain-context>
   295→ *
   296→ * For array-form system prompts, each block becomes a separate text element
   297→ * with cache_control: ephemeral.
   298→ */
   299→export function injectRecentChanges(
   300→  system: SystemPrompt,
   301→  recentChangesXml: string,
   302→): InjectionResult["system"] {
   303→  if (!recentChangesXml) return system ?? "";
   304→
   305→  // Extract urgent-context and context-update blocks from the XML
   306→  const urgentMatch = recentChangesXml.match(/<urgent-context>[\s\S]*?<\/urgent-context>/);
   307→  const updateMatch = recentChangesXml.match(/<context-update>[\s\S]*?<\/context-update>/);
   308→
   309→  const urgentBlock = urgentMatch?.[0] ?? "";
   310→  const updateBlock = updateMatch?.[0] ?? "";
   311→
   312→  if (!urgentBlock && !updateBlock) return system ?? "";
   313→
   314→  if (Array.isArray(system)) {
   315→    const result = [...system];
   316→
   317→    // Find the brain-context block index for insertion ordering
   318→    const brainContextIdx = result.findIndex(
   319→      (block) => typeof block.text === "string" && block.text.includes("<brain-context>"),
   320→    );
   321→
   322→    if (brainContextIdx >= 0) {
   323→      // Insert urgent BEFORE brain-context, update AFTER
   324→      if (updateBlock) {
   325→        result.splice(brainContextIdx + 1, 0, {
   326→          type: "text",
   327→          text: updateBlock,
   328→          cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" },
   329→        });
   330→      }
   331→      if (urgentBlock) {
   332→        result.splice(brainContextIdx, 0, {
   333→          type: "text",
   334→          text: urgentBlock,
   335→          cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" },
   336→        });
   337→      }
   338→    } else {
   339→      // No brain-context block -- append both at end (urgent first, then update)
   340→      if (urgentBlock) {
   341→        result.push({ type: "text", text: urgentBlock, cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" } });
   342→      }
   343→      if (updateBlock) {
   344→        result.push({ type: "text", text: updateBlock, cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" } });
   345→      }
   346→    }
   347→
   348→    return result;
   349→  }
   350→
   351→  if (typeof system === "string") {
   352→    // String-form: find <brain-context> position for ordering
   353→    const brainContextPos = system.indexOf("<brain-context>");
   354→
   355→    if (brainContextPos >= 0) {
   356→      const brainContextEnd = system.indexOf("</brain-context>");
   357→      const afterBrainContext = brainContextEnd >= 0
   358→        ? brainContextEnd + "</brain-context>".length
   359→        : system.length;
   360→
   361→      const before = system.slice(0, brainContextPos);
   362→      const brainSection = system.slice(brainContextPos, afterBrainContext);
   363→      const after = system.slice(afterBrainContext);
   364→
   365→      const parts = [before.trimEnd()];
   366→      if (urgentBlock) parts.push(urgentBlock);
   367→      parts.push(brainSection);
   368→      if (updateBlock) parts.push(updateBlock);
   369→      parts.push(after.trimStart());
   370→
   371→      return parts.filter(Boolean).join("\n\n");
   372→    }
   373→
   374→    // No brain-context -- append both
   375→    const parts = [system];
   376→    if (urgentBlock) parts.push(urgentBlock);
   377→    if (updateBlock) parts.push(updateBlock);
   378→    return parts.join("\n\n");
   379→  }
   380→
   381→  // No existing system prompt -- combine blocks
   382→  const parts: string[] = [];
   383→  if (urgentBlock) parts.push(urgentBlock);
   384→  if (updateBlock) parts.push(updateBlock);
   385→  return parts.join("\n\n");
   386→}
   387→
   388→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   389→// Recent Changes Classification (02-02: time-based, replaces similarity thresholds)
   390→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   391→
   392→const URGENT_AGE_MINUTES = 30;
   393→const RECENT_AGE_HOURS = 24;
   394→
   395→/**
   396→ * Classifies context items by age into urgent-context or context-update.
   397→ *
   398→ * - <= 30 minutes old  -> urgent-context (agent should act on this immediately)
   399→ * - <= 24 hours old    -> context-update (background awareness)
   400→ * - > 24 hours old     -> filtered out (stale)
   401→ *
   402→ * Pure function: takes explicit `now` for deterministic testing.
   403→ * Preserves input order for candidates that pass the age filter.
   404→ */
   405→export function classifyByAge(
   406→  candidates: RecentChangeCandidate[],
   407→  now: Date,
   408→): ClassifiedChange[] {
   409→  const urgentThresholdMs = URGENT_AGE_MINUTES * 60 * 1000;
   410→  const recentThresholdMs = RECENT_AGE_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000;
   411→
   412→  return candidates
   413→    .map((c) => {
   414→      const ageMs = now.getTime() - new Date(c.updatedAt).getTime();
   415→      if (ageMs > recentThresholdMs) return undefined;
   416→      return {
   417→        ...c,
   418→        classification: ageMs <= urgentThresholdMs
   419→          ? "urgent-context" as const
   420→          : "context-update" as const,
   421→      };
   422→    })
   423→    .filter((c): c is ClassifiedChange => c !== undefined);
   424→}
   425→
   426→/**
   427→ * @deprecated Use classifyByAge instead. Kept for backwards compatibility during migration.
   428→ */
   429→export function classifyBySimilarity(
   430→  candidates: RecentChangeCandidate[],
   431→): ClassifiedChange[] {
   432→  return classifyByAge(candidates, new Date());
   433→}
   434→
   435→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   436→// Recent Changes XML Builder (04-01: pure)
   437→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   438→
   439→/**
   440→ * Builds an XML block for recent graph changes classified by urgency.
   441→ *
   442→ * Output structure:
   443→ * <recent-changes>
   444→ *   <urgent-context>
   445→ *     <item type="decision">Use tRPC for all APIs</item>
   446→ *   </urgent-context>
   447→ *   <context-update>
   448→ *     <item type="task">Implement rate limiting</item>
   449→ *   </context-update>
   450→ * </recent-changes>
   451→ */
   452→function escapeXml(text: string): string {
   453→  return text
   454→    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
   455→    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
   456→    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
   457→    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
   458→}
   459→
   460→export function buildRecentChangesXml(changes: ClassifiedChange[]): string {
   461→  if (changes.length === 0) return "";
   462→
   463→  const urgent = changes.filter((c) => c.classification === "urgent-context");
   464→  const updates = changes.filter((c) => c.classification === "context-update");
   465→
   466→  const sections: string[] = [];
   467→
   468→  if (urgent.length > 0) {
   469→    const items = urgent
   470→      .map((c) => `  <item type="${escapeXml(c.table)}">${escapeXml(c.text)}</item>`)
   471→      .join("\n");
   472→    sections.push(`<urgent-context>\n${items}\n</urgent-context>`);
   473→  }
   474→
   475→  if (updates.length > 0) {
   476→    const items = updates
   477→      .map((c) => `  <item type="${escapeXml(c.table)}">${escapeXml(c.text)}</item>`)
   478→      .join("\n");
   479→    sections.push(`<context-update>\n${items}\n</context-update>`);
   480→  }
   481→
   482→  return `<recent-changes>\n${sections.join("\n")}\n</recent-changes>`;
   483→}
   484→
   485→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   486→// Time-Based Recent Changes (02-03: pure functions)
   487→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   488→
   489→/**
   490→ * Row shape returned by time-based entity queries.
   491→ * Used by mapRowsToRecentChanges to produce RecentChangeCandidate[].
   492→ */
   493→export type TimeBasedChangeRow = {
   494→  readonly id: string;
   495→  readonly text: string;
   496→  readonly updated_at: string;
   497→};
   498→
   499→/**
   500→ * Compute a cutoff Date by subtracting windowMs from now.
   501→ * Pure date arithmetic -- no IO.
   502→ */
   503→export function buildTimeWindowCutoff(now: Date, windowMs: number): Date {
   504→  return new Date(now.getTime() - windowMs);
   505→}
   506→
   507→/**
   508→ * Transform raw DB rows into RecentChangeCandidate[].
   509→ *
   510→ * Sets similarity to 1.0 for all items since time-based retrieval
   511→ * does not produce relevance scores -- all items within the window
   512→ * are equally relevant by definition.
   513→ */
   514→export function mapRowsToRecentChanges(
   515→  rows: TimeBasedChangeRow[],
   516→  table: RecentChangeCandidate["table"],
   517→): RecentChangeCandidate[] {
   518→  return rows.map((row) => ({
   519→    id: row.id,
   520→    table,
   521→    text: row.text,
   522→    similarity: 1.0,
   523→    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
   524→  }));
   525→}
   526→
   527→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   528→// Time-Based Recent Changes Adapter (02-03: replaces BM25 search)
   529→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   530→
   531→/**
   532→ * Port signature for fetching recent graph changes by time window.
   533→ * No text query needed -- pure time-ordered retrieval.
   534→ */
   535→export type FetchRecentChangesByTime = (
   536→  workspaceId: string,
   537→  cutoff: Date,
   538→  limit: number,
   539→) => Promise<RecentChangeCandidate[]>;
   540→
   541→type TimeBasedDbRow = {
   542→  id: RecordId;
   543→  text: string;
   544→  updated_at: string;
   545→};
   546→
   547→/**
   548→ * Fetches recent graph entity changes by time window using simple
   549→ * WHERE updated_at > $cutoff queries. No embeddings, no BM25.
   550→ *
   551→ * Results are ordered by updated_at DESC -- most recent first.
   552→ */
   553→export function createFetchRecentChangesByTime(
   554→  surreal: { query: <T>(sql: string, vars?: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<T> },
   555→): FetchRecentChangesByTime {
   556→  return async (
   557→    workspaceId: string,
   558→    cutoff: Date,
   559→    limit: number,
   560→  ): Promise<RecentChangeCandidate[]> => {
   561→    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   562→
   563→    const results = await surreal.query<[TimeBasedDbRow[], TimeBasedDbRow[], TimeBasedDbRow[]]>(
   564→      `SELECT id, summary AS text, updated_at
   565→         FROM decision WHERE workspace = $ws AND updated_at > $cutoff AND status != "superseded"
   566→         ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT $limit;
   567→       SELECT id, title AS text, updated_at
   568→         FROM task WHERE workspace = $ws AND updated_at > $cutoff
   569→         ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT $limit;
   570→       SELECT id, text, updated_at
   571→         FROM observation WHERE workspace = $ws AND updated_at > $cutoff
   572→         ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT $limit;`,
   573→      { ws: workspaceRecord, cutoff: cutoff.toISOString(), limit },
   574→    );
   575→
   576→    function toRecentChanges(
   577→      rows: TimeBasedDbRow[],
   578→      table: RecentChangeCandidate["table"],
   579→    ): RecentChangeCandidate[] {
   580→      return rows.map((row) => ({
   581→        id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   582→        table,
   583→        text: row.text,
   584→        similarity: 1.0,
   585→        updatedAt: row.updated_at,
   586→      }));
   587→    }
   588→
   589→    return [
   590→      ...toRecentChanges(results[0] ?? [], "decision"),
   591→      ...toRecentChanges(results[1] ?? [], "task"),
   592→      ...toRecentChanges(results[2] ?? [], "observation"),
   593→    ];
   594→  };
   595→}
   596→
   597→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   598→// Recent Changes BM25 Search (04-01 / 02-01: adapter boundary -- DB side effect)
   599→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   600→
   601→/**
   602→ * Port signature for searching recent graph changes by BM25 text relevance.
   603→ * No embedding API calls -- uses fulltext indexes with recency filtering.
   604→ */
   605→export type SearchRecentChanges = (
   606→  queryText: string,
   607→  workspaceId: string,
   608→  lastRequestAt: Date,
   609→) => Promise<RecentChangeCandidate[]>;
   610→
   611→type Bm25RecentRow = {
   612→  id: RecordId;
   613→  text: string;
   614→  score: number;
   615→  updated_at: string;
   616→};
   617→
   618→/**
   619→ * Searches for recent graph entity changes relevant to a text query using BM25.
   620→ *
   621→ * Replaces KNN vector search (02-01): no embedding generation needed.
   622→ * Uses BM25 fulltext indexes on decision.summary, task.title, observation.text.
   623→ */
   624→export function createSearchRecentChanges(
   625→  surreal: { query: <T>(sql: string, vars?: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<T> },
   626→): SearchRecentChanges {
   627→  return async (
   628→    queryText: string,
   629→    workspaceId: string,
   630→    lastRequestAt: Date,
   631→  ): Promise<RecentChangeCandidate[]> => {
   632→    // BM25 @N@ does AND matching — use OR across separate predicates per term.
   633→    // See: https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealdb/models/full-text-search
   634→    const termList = extractSearchTerms(queryText)
   635→      .split(" ")
   636→      .filter((t) => t.length > 0);
   637→    if (termList.length === 0) return [];
   638→
   639→    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   640→    const limit = 20;
   641→
   642→    // Build OR-predicate clauses for each table
   643→    const scoreExpr = termList.map((_, i) => `search::score(${i})`).join(" + ");
   644→    const bindings: Record<string, unknown> = { ws: workspaceRecord, since: lastRequestAt, limit };
   645→    termList.forEach((term, i) => { bindings[`t${i}`] = term; });
   646→
   647→    const matchClause = (field: string) =>
   648→      termList.map((_, i) => `${field} @${i}@ $t${i}`).join(" OR ");
   649→
   650→    const sql = `SELECT id, summary AS text, ${scoreExpr} AS score, updated_at
   651→         FROM decision WHERE (${matchClause("summary")}) AND workspace = $ws AND updated_at > $since AND status != "superseded"
   652→         ORDER BY score DESC LIMIT $limit;
   653→       SELECT id, title AS text, ${scoreExpr} AS score, updated_at
   654→         FROM task WHERE (${matchClause("title")}) AND workspace = $ws AND updated_at > $since
   655→         ORDER BY score DESC LIMIT $limit;
   656→       SELECT id, text, ${scoreExpr} AS score, updated_at
   657→         FROM observation WHERE (${matchClause("text")}) AND workspace = $ws AND updated_at > $since
   658→         ORDER BY score DESC LIMIT $limit;`;
   659→
   660→    const results = await surreal.query<[Bm25RecentRow[], Bm25RecentRow[], Bm25RecentRow[]]>(
   661→      sql,
   662→      bindings,
   663→    );
   664→
   665→    function toRrfItems(
   666→      rows: Bm25RecentRow[],
   667→      table: RecentChangeCandidate["table"],
   668→    ): RrfItem<Omit<RecentChangeCandidate, "similarity">>[] {
   669→      return rows.map((row) => ({
   670→        _rrfKey: `${table}:${(row.id as RecordId).id as string}`,
   671→        id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   672→        table,
   673→        text: row.text,
   674→        updatedAt: row.updated_at,
   675→      }));
   676→    }
   677→
   678→    const fused = applyRrf(
   679→      [
   680→        toRrfItems(results[0] ?? [], "decision"),
   681→        toRrfItems(results[1] ?? [], "task"),
   682→        toRrfItems(results[2] ?? [], "observation"),
   683→      ],
   684→      limit,
   685→    );
   686→
   687→    return fused.map(({ rrfScore, ...rest }) => ({
   688→      ...rest,
   689→      similarity: rrfScore,
   690→    }));
   691→  };
   692→}
   693→
   694→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   695→// BM25 Context Search (02-01: adapter boundary -- DB side effect)
   696→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   697→
   698→/**
   699→ * Port signature for searching context candidates by BM25 text relevance.
   700→ */
   701→export type SearchContextByBm25 = (
   702→  queryText: string,
   703→  workspaceId: string,
   704→  limit: number,
   705→) => Promise<Bm25ContextCandidate[]>;
   706→
   707→type Bm25ContextRow = {
   708→  id: RecordId;
   709→  text: string;
   710→  score: number;
   711→  updated_at: string;
   712→};
   713→
   714→/**
   715→ * Searches for context candidates (decisions, learnings, observations) using BM25 fulltext.
   716→ *
   717→ * Replaces embedding cosine similarity ranking (02-01).
   718→ * Returns candidates with BM25 scores and timestamps for recency-weighted ranking.
   719→ */
   720→export function createSearchContextByBm25(
   721→  surreal: { query: <T>(sql: string, vars?: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<T> },
   722→): SearchContextByBm25 {
   723→  return async (
   724→    queryText: string,
   725→    workspaceId: string,
   726→    limit: number,
   727→  ): Promise<Bm25ContextCandidate[]> => {
   728→    const trimmed = queryText.trim();
   729→    if (trimmed.length === 0) return [];
   730→
   731→    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   732→
   733→    const results = await surreal.query<[Bm25ContextRow[], Bm25ContextRow[], Bm25ContextRow[]]>(
   734→      `SELECT id, summary AS text, search::score(1) AS score, updated_at
   735→         FROM decision WHERE summary @1@ $query AND workspace = $ws AND status = 'confirmed'
   736→         ORDER BY score DESC LIMIT $limit;
   737→       SELECT id, text, search::score(1) AS score, updated_at
   738→         FROM learning WHERE text @1@ $query AND workspace = $ws AND status = 'active'
   739→         ORDER BY score DESC LIMIT $limit;
   740→       SELECT id, text, search::score(1) AS score, updated_at
   741→         FROM observation WHERE text @1@ $query AND workspace = $ws AND status = 'open' AND severity IN ['conflict', 'warning'] AND source_agent != 'llm-proxy'
   742→         ORDER BY score DESC LIMIT $limit;`,
   743→      { ws: workspaceRecord, limit, query: trimmed },
   744→    );
   745→
   746→    function toRrfItems(
   747→      rows: Bm25ContextRow[],
   748→      type: Bm25ContextCandidate["type"],
   749→    ): RrfItem<Omit<Bm25ContextCandidate, "bm25Score">>[] {
   750→      return rows.map((row) => ({
   751→        _rrfKey: `${type}:${(row.id as RecordId).id as string}`,
   752→        id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   753→        type,
   754→        text: row.text,
   755→        updatedAt: row.updated_at,
   756→      }));
   757→    }
   758→
   759→    const fused = applyRrf(
   760→      [
   761→        toRrfItems(results[0] ?? [], "decision"),
   762→        toRrfItems(results[1] ?? [], "learning"),
   763→        toRrfItems(results[2] ?? [], "observation"),
   764→      ],
   765→      limit,
   766→    );
   767→
   768→    return fused.map(({ rrfScore, ...rest }) => ({
   769→      ...rest,
   770→      bm25Score: rrfScore,
   771→    }));
   772→  };
   773→}
   774→

     1→/**
     2→ * Context Cache -- In-memory TTL cache for workspace candidate pools
     3→ *
     4→ * Stores workspace context candidates (decisions, learnings, observations)
     5→ * with embeddings. Refreshed on TTL expiry. NOT a module-level singleton --
     6→ * created per proxy handler instance via createContextCache().
     7→ *
     8→ * Port: createContextCache(ttlSeconds) -> ContextCache
     9→ */
    10→
    11→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→// Types
    13→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→export type CandidateItem = {
    16→  readonly id: string;
    17→  readonly type: "decision" | "learning" | "observation";
    18→  readonly text: string;
    19→  readonly weight: number;
    20→};
    21→
    22→export type CachedCandidatePool = {
    23→  readonly decisions: CandidateItem[];
    24→  readonly learnings: CandidateItem[];
    25→  readonly observations: CandidateItem[];
    26→  readonly populatedAt: number;
    27→};
    28→
    29→export type ContextCache = {
    30→  get(workspaceId: string): CachedCandidatePool | undefined;
    31→  set(workspaceId: string, pool: CachedCandidatePool): void;
    32→  has(workspaceId: string): boolean;
    33→};
    34→
    35→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→// Factory
    37→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38→
    39→export function createContextCache(ttlSeconds: number): ContextCache {
    40→  // Per-handler-instance cache (created via factory in createAnthropicProxyHandler).
    41→  // TTL provides eventual consistency with the graph — stale reads are acceptable
    42→  // since context injection is best-effort. Cache stampede on cold start is fine:
    43→  // concurrent requests for the same workspace may each populate, but the last
    44→  // write wins and subsequent reads serve from cache. Workspace cardinality is
    45→  // low enough that unbounded Map growth is not a concern.
    46→  const store = new Map<string, CachedCandidatePool>();
    47→  const ttlMs = ttlSeconds * 1000;
    48→
    49→  function isExpired(pool: CachedCandidatePool): boolean {
    50→    return Date.now() - pool.populatedAt > ttlMs;
    51→  }
    52→
    53→  return {
    54→    get(workspaceId: string): CachedCandidatePool | undefined {
    55→      const cached = store.get(workspaceId);
    56→      if (!cached) return undefined;
    57→      if (isExpired(cached)) {
    58→        store.delete(workspaceId);
    59→        return undefined;
    60→      }
    61→      return cached;
    62→    },
    63→
    64→    set(workspaceId: string, pool: CachedCandidatePool): void {
    65→      store.set(workspaceId, pool);
    66→    },
    67→
    68→    has(workspaceId: string): boolean {
    69→      const cached = store.get(workspaceId);
    70→      if (!cached) return false;
    71→      if (isExpired(cached)) {
    72→        store.delete(workspaceId);
    73→        return false;
    74→      }
    75→      return true;
    76→    },
    77→  };
    78→}
    79→

[pure]\n```\n\nThe batch query currently fetches: `id, workspace, status, created_at` from each referenced record. For authorship independence, the query also needs to fetch the author/source agent field.\n\n## Authorship Fields by Entity Type\n- decision: `source_agent` field (the agent that created/confirmed it)\n- task: `source_agent` or `created_by` field — need to check schema\n- observation: `source_agent` field\n\nRead the SurrealDB schema (`schema/surreal-schema.surql`) to find the exact authorship fields for each entity type.\n\n## Risk Tier Requirements (from architecture-design.md)\n- Low risk (0-30): 1 ref of any type, 0 independent authors\n- Medium risk (31-70): 2+ refs with decision/task type, 1 independent author\n- High risk (71-100): 3+ refs with decision AND task/observation, 2 independent authors\n\n## Key Implementation Notes\n1. The `verifyEvidence` function needs a new parameter: `requestingAgentId` (or similar) to compare authorship against\n2. The batch query needs to also fetch the authorship field (e.g. `source_agent`) for each record\n3. `classifyQueryResults` (or a new pure function) computes `independent_author_count` by comparing each evidence author against the requesting agent\n4. `EvidenceVerificationResult` already has `independent_author_count?: number` and `tier_met?: boolean` fields (added in step 03-01)\n5. The `evaluateIntent` function in `authorizer.ts` needs to pass the requesting agent info through. However, `authorizer.ts` is NOT in files_to_modify for this step — focus on the evidence-verification module. The integration wiring (passing requester identity through) may need to happen in a later step or you may need to add the parameter and document that it needs to be wired.\n\n## Evidence Types (current from evidence-types.ts)\n```typescript\nexport type EvidenceVerificationResult = {\n  readonly verified_count: number;\n  readonly total_count: number;\n  readonly failed_refs?: string[];\n  readonly verification_time_ms: number;\n  readonly warnings?: string[];\n  readonly enforcement_mode: EvidenceEnforcementMode;\n  readonly independent_author_count?: number;\n  readonly tier_met?: boolean;\n};\n```\n\n## Evidence Constants (current)\n```typescript\nexport const EVIDENCE_TABLE_ALLOWLIST = new Set([\"decision\", \"task\", \"feature\", \"project\", \"observation\", \"policy\", \"objective\", \"learning\", \"git_commit\"]);\nexport const VALID_EVIDENCE_STATUSES: Record<string, ReadonlySet<string>> = { ... };\nexport const EVIDENCE_SHORTFALL_PENALTY = 20;\nexport const LOW_TIER_MIN_COUNT = 1;\n```\n\nNeeds: tier requirement definitions including independent author counts per risk tier.\n\n## EvidenceQueryRow Type (current)\n```typescript\nexport type EvidenceQueryRow = {\n  id: RecordId;\n  workspace: RecordId;\n  status?: string;\n  created_at?: Date;\n};\n```\nNeeds: `source_agent?: string` field added for authorship tracking.\n\n## Test Kit Helpers Available\nThe test file imports: `createMultipleAgentIdentities`, `createTestIdentity`, `createEvidenceDecision`, `createEvidenceTask`, `createEvidenceObservation`, `createIntentWithEvidence`, `submitIntent`, `waitForIntentStatus`, `getEvidenceVerification`, `setWorkspaceEnforcementMode`. These are in `intent-evidence-test-kit.ts`.\n\n## Intent Evaluation Integration\nThe `evaluateIntent` in `authorizer.ts` currently calls `verifyEvidence` but doesn't pass a requesting agent ID. For the acceptance test to work end-to-end, you'll need to check how intent-evaluation.ts wires things and potentially update the verifyEvidence call to pass the requester identity. Read `app/src/server/intent/intent-evaluation.ts` to understand the wiring.\n\n**IMPORTANT**: The test creates intents via `createIntentWithEvidence` which takes an `identityId` parameter. The evaluation pipeline needs to thread this through to the verification. You may need to modify `intent-evaluation.ts` and/or `authorizer.ts` to pass the requester identity — add these to your implementation scope if needed to make the acceptance test pass.\n\n## Git Conventions\n- Always use `--no-verify` and `-s` (GPG sign) when committing\n- Commit message format: `feat(intent): description` with Step-ID trailer\n\n# TDD_PHASES\nExecute in order:\n0. PREPARE - Load context, read the files to modify and related files (evidence-verification.ts, evidence-constants.ts, evidence-types.ts, intent-evaluation.ts, authorizer.ts, schema/surreal-schema.surql for authorship fields). GREEN - Implement:\n   a. Add authorship field to batch query (fetch source_agent alongside existing fields)\n   b. Add tier requirement constants (risk tier thresholds with min_refs, required_types, min_independent_authors)\n   d. Wire into verifyEvidence pipeline\n   e. Thread requester identity through intent-evaluation → evaluateIntent → verifyEvidence if needed\n   Run FULL test suite after GREEN.\n4. COMMIT - Stage and commit\n   ```\n   feat(intent): implement authorship independence check in evidence verification pipeline\n   \n   Step-ID: 03-03\n   ```\n\n# QUALITY_GATES\n- All tests pass before COMMIT\n- No skipped phases without blocked_by reason\n\n# OUTCOME_RECORDING\nAfter ACTUALLY EXECUTING each phase, record via DES CLI:\n\n    PYTHONPATH=$HOME/.claude/lib/python python3 -m des.cli.log_phase \\\n      --project-dir docs/feature/intent-evidence/deliver \\\n      --step-id 03-03 \\\n      --phase {PHASE_NAME} \\\n      --status EXECUTED \\\n      --data PASS\n\nFor SKIPPED phases:\n\n    PYTHONPATH=$HOME/.claude/lib/python python3 -m des.cli.log_phase \\\n      --project-dir docs/feature/intent-evidence/deliver \\\n      --step-id 03-03 \\\n      --phase {PHASE_NAME} \\\n      --status SKIPPED \\\n      --data \"NOT_APPLICABLE: reason\"\n\n# BOUNDARY_RULES\n- Primary files: evidence-verification.ts, evidence-constants.ts, evidence-types.ts\n- May also modify: intent-evaluation.ts, authorizer.ts (for wiring requester identity through)\n- Do not load roadmap.json\n- Do not modify execution-log.json structure (append only)\n\n# TIMEOUT_INSTRUCTION\nTarget: 30 turns max. So `fetchJson` takes a full URL. But the test passes `fetchJson(baseUrl, path, ...)`. That's wrong -- it needs to be `fetchJson(baseUrl + path, opts)`. Wait, the test uses `fetchJson(baseUrl, \`/api/...\`, {...})` -- but `fetchJson` only takes 2 params. The test is calling it with 3 arguments.

Looking at the test more carefully:

```ts
const policyResponse = await fetchJson(baseUrl, `/api/workspaces/${workspace.workspaceId}/policies`, {
```

But `fetchJson` signature is `(url: string, init?: RequestInit)`. This is being called with 3 args -- the second arg (the path) becomes `init` (wrong type), and the third arg is ignored. That's why it fails -- it's fetching `baseUrl` (the server root) with no path.

I need to fix the test to use the correct pattern. Let me look at how other tests call policy endpoints.

   115→  const content: Record<string, unknown> = {
   116→    title: opts.title,
   117→    version: 1,
   118→    status: opts.status ?? "draft",
   119→    selector: opts.selector ?? {},
   120→    rules: opts.rules,
   121→    human_veto_required: opts.human_veto_required ?? false,

   128→  if (opts.max_ttl) content.max_ttl = opts.max_ttl;

   131→    policy: policyRecord,
   132→    content,
   133→  });
   134→
   135→  return { policyId, policyRecord };
   136→}
   137→
   138→export async function activatePolicy(
   139→  surreal: Surreal,
   140→  policyId: string,
   141→  creatorId: string,
   142→  workspaceId: string,
   143→): Promise<void> {

   150→      UPDATE $policy SET status = 'active', updated_at = time::now();
   151→      RELATE $creator->governing->$policy SET created_at = time::now();
   152→      RELATE $policy->protects->$workspace SET created_at = time::now();
   153→    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

## ADVERSARIAL REVIEW: Intent-Evidence Feature

**Review Status**: Comprehensive review complete

---

## SUMMARY

The intent-evidence feature delivers evidence-backed intent authorization with evidence verification, risk-based enforcement, and maturity-driven mode transitions. The implementation follows the pure-function-at-effect-boundary pattern consistently, with strong type safety and separation of concerns.

**Overall Assessment**: **APPROVED** with noted observations.

---

## CRITICAL FINDINGS

### NONE DETECTED

No blockers, no testing theater, no test modifications, no security violations detected.

---

## STRENGTHS

1. **Pure Function Pipeline** (evidence-verification.ts:1-461)
   - Exemplary separation: parsing → batch query → classification → result building
   - All pure functions are genuinely pure (no IO, no side effects)
   - Single effect boundary at `batchQueryEvidence()` — all other work is testable without DB
   - **file**: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/phoenix-v2/app/src/server/intent/evidence-verification.ts`

2. **Comprehensive Test Coverage**
   - 50+ test cases across unit and acceptance suites
   - No tautological assertions — all tests assert on meaningful outcomes
   - Unit tests on pure functions: `parseEvidenceRef`, `classifyQueryResults`, `evaluateRiskTierRequirements`, `countIndependentAuthors`, `buildVerificationResult`
   - Acceptance tests trace complete workflows through entry points (SurrealDB intent creation, evaluation pipeline, feed rendering)
   - **file**: Tests across `tests/unit/intent/` and `tests/acceptance/intent-evidence/`

3. **Defense-in-Depth Input Validation**
   - Evidence refs validated at MCP handler boundary (validateEvidenceRefs): table allowlist check + format validation
   - Table allowlist constant (EVIDENCE_TABLE_ALLOWLIST) guards against unsupported types
   - RecordId escaping correctly handled (stripRecordIdEscaping)
   - **files**: 
     - `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/phoenix-v2/app/src/server/mcp/create-intent-handler.ts:104-172`
     - `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/phoenix-v2/app/src/server/intent/evidence-verification.ts:45-58`

4. **Port-to-Port Testing Compliance**
   - Acceptance tests drive through SurrealDB (driving port: intent creation with evidence_refs)
   - Evidence verification tested via pure functions + integration tests
   - No internal class testing; domain entities (Decision, Task) tested indirectly through service interfaces
   - **file**: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/phoenix-v2/tests/acceptance/intent-evidence/milestone-1-core-verification.test.ts:51-138`

5. **Type-Safe RecordId Handling**
   - `ParsedEvidenceRef` type encapsulates table + id + RecordId object
   - No string-based polymorphic IDs in queries; all refs are strongly typed
   - Escaping logic centralized in `stripRecordIdEscaping`
   - **file**: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/phoenix-v2/app/src/server/intent/evidence-types.ts`

6. **Maturity Transition Logic (Pure, Composable)**
   - `shouldTransitionToHardEnforcement()` and `shouldTransitionToSoftEnforcement()` are pure functions
   - Lazy evaluation model (transitions checked at intent evaluation time, not on every write)
   - Bootstrap → Soft → Hard progression correctly enforced
   - **file**: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/phoenix-v2/app/src/server/intent/maturity-transition.ts`

7. **Risk Router Integration**
   - Evidence penalty computed as separate pure function (`computeEvidencePenalty`)
   - Soft enforcement applies penalty only when verification < required; hard enforcement short-circuits before LLM
   - **file**: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/phoenix-v2/app/src/server/intent/risk-router.ts:15-56`

## MEDIUM-SEVERITY OBSERVATIONS

### M1: Workspace Settings Query Over-Fetching

**Location**: `app/src/server/intent/intent-evaluation.ts:143-150`

**Pattern**: Query fetches `evidence_enforcement`, `min_evidence_age_minutes`, `evidence_enforcement_threshold` in a single SELECT statement. This is actually correct per the project guidance (batch queries), but the query is repeated later in the function for maturity counts.

**Observation** (not a blocker): The function performs multiple separate `.query()` calls to the same workspace record. Consider batching if performance is critical. Current approach is acceptable for correctness.

---

### M2: Verification Result Optional Fields

**Location**: `app/src/server/intent/evidence-types.ts:22-31`

**Pattern**: `failed_refs?`, `warnings?`, `independent_author_count?`, `tier_met?` are optional fields.

**Observation**: This follows project convention (optional fields, not null). When `failed_refs` is an empty array, it's omitted from the result (see `buildVerificationResult`, line 353). This is correct — absence represents the happy path.

---

### M3: Test with Generic Role

**Location**: `tests/unit/intent/authorizer.test.ts:62`

**Pattern**: makeInput uses `requesterType: "agent"` without parametrization.

## EXTERNAL VALIDITY CHECK

**Driving Ports**:
- Intent creation with evidence_refs: Driven through SurrealDB `CREATE intent` (direct, not HTTP in walking skeleton)
- Evidence verification: Evaluated as part of intent evaluation pipeline
- Workspace enforcement settings: Set via SurrealDB UPDATE

**Entry Points**: 
- MCP `create_intent` handler (app/src/server/mcp/create-intent-handler.ts) invokes intent creation flow
- Intent evaluation triggered via SurrealDB EVENT webhook (policy gate → evaluatePendingIntent)

**Wiring Verified**: 
- create_intent_handler parses evidence_refs, validates allowlist, creates intent
- authorizer.ts:evaluateIntent() calls verifyEvidence() with workspace enforcement mode
- risk_router applies penalty based on verification result
- Feed queries (feed-queries.ts) include evidence summary for governance visibility

**Status**: PASS — Features are invocable through entry points, not isolated.

## TESTING THEATER DETECTION

**Scan**: Applied to all test files for 7 patterns (zero-assertion, tautological, mock-dominated, circular, always-green, fully-mocked SUT, assertion-free, implementation-mirroring, misleading names, hardcoded oracle).

**Result**: **NO THEATER PATTERNS DETECTED**

Examples of strong tests:
1. **evidence-verification.test.ts:69-83** — "marks all refs as verified when all records exist in the same workspace"
   - Constructs parsed refs + query rows with specific workspace
   - Calls classifyQueryResults
   - Asserts verifiedCount == 2, failedRefs empty, warnings empty
   - ✓ Behavioral assertion

2. **evidence-verification.test.ts:100-113** — "marks records from wrong workspace as failed"
   - Sets up different workspace IDs
   - Expects verification to FAIL for cross-workspace ref
   - Asserts verifiedCount == 0, failedRefs contains the ID, warnings mention workspace
   - ✓ Tests observable failure condition

3. **maturity-transition.test.ts:11-19** — "returns true when both counts meet threshold"
   - Inputs at exact threshold boundary
   - Expects true result
   - ✓ Boundary value tested

4. **risk-router.test.ts:24-26** — "returns auto_approve when APPROVE and risk_score is 0"
   - Asserts route == "auto_approve"
   - ✓ Clear behavioral assertion

## ARCHITECTURE COMPLIANCE

**Hexagonal Architecture**: PASS

- **Domain Layer**: Evidence types and pure verification functions are domain-neutral (not infrastructure-specific)
- **Application Layer**: `verifyEvidence()` orchestrates pure functions with single effect boundary
- **Infrastructure Layer**: SurrealDB queries isolated in `batchQueryEvidence()` and intent-queries.ts
- **Ports**: 
  - Driving: Intent creation handler + evaluation event
  - Driven: SurrealDB (mocked in unit tests, real in acceptance)

**Dependency Direction**: CORRECT

- No circular imports detected
- intent-verification depends on evidence-types, evidence-constants (downward)
- authorizer depends on evidence-verification (correct: orchestrator depends on verification)
- risk-router is stateless (no dependencies on internal state)

---

## SECURITY ASSESSMENT

**Injection Vulnerabilities**: NONE DETECTED

- Evidence refs parsed with strict table allowlist (EVIDENCE_TABLE_ALLOWLIST)
- Table names validated before RecordId construction
- SurrealDB queries use parameterized bindings (`$r0`, `$r1`, etc.) — no string interpolation
- RecordId objects prevent SQL-like injection

**Workspace Scope Enforcement**: CORRECT

- `normalizeWorkspaceId()` extracts workspace ID consistently from RecordId.id
- classifyQueryResults compares normalized workspace IDs
- Cross-workspace evidence fails scope check before any other processing

**Temporal Ordering**: CORRECT

- Evidence created_at compared against intent created_at
- Evidence created AFTER intent fails temporal_violation check
- Prevents backdated evidence fabrication

---
